### POWER WINDOW SWITCH REPLACEMENT REAR DOOR (CHEVROLET)
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_8b92529a` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Removal Procedure

1. Using a flat-bladed tool, remove the window switch bezel from the door trim panel

![](images/891374f5a1e4558912b14d60fa5bb34ee2d7003ba174a9eec4eaca1a2a295d6f.jpg)
Fig. 14: Replacing Rear Door Power Window Switch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Disconnect the electrical connector from the window switch

![](images/1bd1d5c20a9e456c3ffd424bf61e8675a92c97f1a0e2f375cd25a486659e8067.jpg)

3. Release the retaining clips that attach the power window switch to the bezel.
4. Remove the switch from the bezel

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6932ba8f` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/e96d832fe3fe32a9483bb82537c34ffef03a49200d9bb8b17b50f52065395741.jpg)
Fig. 15: Replacing Rear Door Power Window Switch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the switch to the bezel. Ensure that the retainers are fully seated.
2. Connect the electrical connector to the window switch.
3. Install the window switch bezel to the door trim panel.
4. Ensure that the window switch bezel retaining clips are fully seated.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_276aad46` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/e11aaa4cbfce46b45a8813179f48db16f5b176fbd178f64cd6d3b73698641ecc.jpg)
Fig. 16: Replacing Door Lock and Side Window Switch Driver Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Lift up on the front edge of the switch panel in order to release the front retaining clip.
2. Lift up on the rear edge of the switch panel in order to release the 2 rear retaining clips.
3. If equipped, disconnect the electrical connectors from the driver door module (1).
4. If replacing only the driver door module, remove the module from the door trim panel.
5. Disconnect the remaining electrical connectors from the switch panel
6. If replacing only the switch panel, retain the driver door module for transfer.
7. Remove the switch panel assembly from the vehicle

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_38b97359` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/95e2fad72c7fa17e18df5809dca4dbc73044b63ef82b8594bbb60709cc9b7499.jpg)
Fig. 17: Replacing Door Lock and Side Window Switch Driver Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the driver door module（l) to the door trim panel.
2. Connect the electrical connector to the driver door module.
3. If replacing the switch panel, connect the remaining electrical connectors.

### IMPORTANT: When replacing the driver door module, the set up procedure must be performed.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_b803f456` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. If replacing the driver door module, program the driver door module Refer to Service

Programming System (SPS) in Programming and Personalization Description and Operation in Personalization.

5. Install the switch panel to the door trim panel, ensuring the front and rear retaining clips are fully seated.

SWITCH REPLACEMENT DOOR LOCK AND SIDE WINDOW PASSENGER

Removal Procedure

![](images/7c9448cae3f7d731612c1c1195381040501fff42304d057ed8df9e7963698495.jpg)
Fig. 18: Replacing Door Lock and Side Window Switch Passenger Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Lift up on the front edge of the switch panel in order to release the front retaining clip.
2. Lift up on the rear edge of the switch panel in order to release the 2 rear retaining clips

3. If equipped, disconnect the electrical connectors from the passenger door module (1).

4. If replacing only the passenger door module, remove the module from the door trim
panel.
5. Disconnect the remaining electrical connectors from the switch panel.
6. If replacing only the switch panel, retain the passenger door module for transfer.
7. Remove the switch panel assembly from the vehicle.

### Fig. 19: Replacing Door Lock and Side Window Switch Passenger Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_dc1e59b1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the passenger door module (1) to the door trim panel.

![](images/1d1e75acb816b1b6e198254f2560d873770d912ccdc6dd9f6cf689b1f4dcf512.jpg)

2. Connect the electrical connector to the passenger door module.
3. If replacing the switch panel, connect the remaining electrical connectors.

### IMPORTANT: When replacing the passenger door module, the set up procedure must be performed.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ee0b07fb` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. If replacing the passenger door module, program the passenger door module. Refer to Service Programming System (SPS) in Programming.
5. Install the switch panel to the door trim panel, ensuring the front and rear retaining clips are fully seated.

### Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_5c15cdc6` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Carefully use a flat-bladed tool in order to release the outside mirror switch retaining tabs from the trim panel.
2. Remove the outside mirror switch from the trim panel.
3. Disconnect the electrical connector from the power folding mirror switch.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ff4df4dd` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/7ae2d64b1d4e814e7a8d4888f53f5d28d598027c42015ad3f716f2ee9376eec6.jpg)
Fig. 21: Replacing Power Mirror Switch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Connect the electrical connector to the power folding mirror switch.
2. Position the power folding mirror switch to the door trim panel.
3. Apply downward pressure to the power folding mirror switch, ensuring the retaining

tabs are fully seated.

### DOOR HANDLE BEZEL REPLACEMENT
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_96a9337d` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Removal Procedu re

![](images/2b6a2aaf86a9bd0cbab51846f865d96536a8a5f6b84c98f0fac0d4fe6dc9361b.jpg)
Fig. 22: Replacing Door Handle Bezel Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Remove the door trim panel from the door. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door or Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Release the 4 tabs retaining the bezel to the trim panel.
3. Remove the bezel from the trim panel.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_31b36d11` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/af1fddec0e8de5ec36f0bab2b99d0cacc48e08bda7b862a498316c7f1032bc18.jpg)
Fig. 23: Replacing Door Handle Bezel Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the bezel to the door, ensuring that all 4 retaining tabs are fully seated 2. Install the door trim panel to the door. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door or Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_67c7b736` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the accessory switch panel. Refer to Switch Replacement Door Lock and Side Window Driver.

![](images/d52ab665da9b9583219e997828bd74957e235c0836c3746b23fe609779e50ebe.jpg)

### Fig. 24: Replacing Side Front Door Trim Panel Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_618d978a` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

2. Remove the 2 trim panel retaining bolts.
3. Use the J36796 in order to release the 9 trim panel fasteners from the door.
4. Remove the trim panel from the door.
5. Remove the inside door handle bezel. Refer to Door Handle Bezel Replacement.
6. Remove the outside rear View mirror (OR VM) switch, if equipped. Refer to Power
Mirror Switch Replacement.

### Fig. 25: Replacing Side Front Door Trim Panel Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_4827d0dd` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the ORVM switch, if equipped. Refer to Power MirrorSwitch Replacement.

2. Install the inside door handle bezel. Refer to Door Handle Bezel Replacement.

3. Install the door trim panel to the door, as follows:

1. Install the panel on the door by placing the upper flange into the belt seal lip.
2. Install the rear upper retainer first.
3. Install the forward upper retainer second.
4. Install the remaining seven retainers.
5. Install the forward trim panel retaining bolt first. Do not tighten the bolt.
6. Install the rearward trim panel retaining bolt second
7. Tighten the two retaining bolts.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_40fb8746` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8. Tighten: Tighten the bolts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

4. Install the accessory switch panel Refer to Switch Replacement Door Lock and Side Window Driver.

TRIM PANEL REPLACEMENT- SIDE REAR DOOR

Tools Rcquircd

J 36796 Clip Removal Tool

Removal Procedurc

![](images/2ce828f56d03379dce1ac7739e3d95df2d224218b3441d2ff578e41e26dc5c19.jpg)
Fig. 26: Replacing Side Rear Door Trim Panel Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Remove the cover from the trim panel
2. Remove the 2 retaining screws from the trim panel.
3. Remove the power window switch. Refer to Power Window Switch Replacement -
Rear Door (Chevrolet).
4. Use the J36796 in order to release the fasteners that retain the trim panel to the door.
5. Remove the trim panel from the door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_24c0ca09` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/225dcd1e772bf3b439e4f61b859cd15eef81138979510c002ddce65042c8230b.jpg)
Fig. 27: Replacing Side Rear Door Trim Panel Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the trim panel to the door, ensuring that all the retaining tabs are fully seated. 2. Install the power window switch. Refer toPower Window Switch Replacement - Rear Door (Chevrolet).

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_b2739167` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Install the 2 retaining screws to the trim panel.

Tighten: Tighten the screws to 2.5 N.m (22 1b in).

![](images/bdd0ef47e42e52acdca7b2c7194151b3d71c2821539489f0c93249fead16a29a.jpg)

4. Install the cover to the trim panel.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_dea652a9` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
2. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement ■ Front Door in Entertainment.

![](images/e261b597a66677ade9e4c9c172adeaac827db0932d5976a81afd5d875749cff3.jpg)

### Fig. 28: Replacing Front Door Water Deflector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_330dca5f` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Remove the outside rear view mirror (ORVM) wire harness from the door.
4. Carefully remove the water deflector from the door by releasing the adhesive backing.

### Fig. 29: Replacing Front Door Water Deflector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_f3ff48ab` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Position the water deflector to the door.
2. Press the water deflector to the door. Ensure that the water deflector forms a proper seal
around the surface of the door panel.
3. Install the OR VM wire harness to the door.
4. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
5. Install the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ba2e72bc` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Open the door.
2. Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

![](images/6477cb73d8f27e115bfaf0c03df803b9302859589e45f1603b6ecbb5153ae05a.jpg)

### Fig. 30: Replacing Rear Door Water Deflector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_1a9027fe` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Carefully remove the water deflector from the door by releasing the adhesive backing.

Installation Procedure

![](images/fe6a89aa9fc6ef0d1e55e701ab17ca0bb960d5a76c05470d0fa7e1c85ad6d19b.jpg)

### Fig. 31: Replacing Rear Door Water Deflector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_36c9482a` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Position the water deflector to the door.
2. Press the water deflector to the door. Ensure that the water deflector forms a proper seal around the surface of the door panel.
3. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_01165241` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Disable the supplemental inflatable restraint (SIR) system Refer to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 2 and to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 6 in SIR.
2. Remove the accessory switch panel. Refer to Switch Replacement Door Lock and Side Window Driver.
3. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
4. Remove the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Front Door Window Belt Outer.
5. Remove the inside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Inside.
6. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
7. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
8. Remove the outside rear view mirror (ORVM). Refer to Mirror Replacement.
9. Remove the window. Refer to Window Replacement - Front Door.
10. Remove the weatherstrip run channel. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement - Front.
11. Remove the window regulator. Refer to Window Regulator Replacement Front Door.
12. Remove the door latch assembly. Refer to Latch Replacement Front Door.
13. Remove the side door SIR sensor. Refer to Inflatable Restraint Side Impact Sensor Replacement in SIR.
14. Remove the outside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement Front Outside.
15. Remove the check link. Refer to Door Check Link Replacement.
16. Remove the wire harness from the door. Refer to Wiring Harness Replacement - Front Door.

![](images/e39c8642d8f05e45a70c9192a165f8a4c9d934dfbc38bd5b50ad4ecffaf535b3.jpg)
Fig. 32: Identifying Door To Hinge Plate Bolts Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

17. Remove the bolts that retain the door to the hinge plates.
18. Remove the door from the vehicle with an assistant.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_2a2c4978` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/00362d6313ab45b3bccdabd80b31975ff8aaa61df6163d07498e2ab7e53d54d9.jpg)
Fig. 33: Identifying Door To Hinge Plate Bolts Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the door to the vehicle with al assistant.

NOTE: Refer toFastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

2. Apply Loctite? Blue GM P/N 12345382, or Canadian P/N 10953489, or equivalent to the fastener prior to installation.
3. Install the bolts that retain the door to the hinge plates.
4 Install the wire harness to the door. Refer to Wiring Harness Replacement- Front Door.
5. Install the check link. Refer to Door Check Link Replacement.
6. Install the outside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Front Outside.
7. Install the side door SIR sensor. Refer to Inflatable Restraint Side Impact Sensor Replacement in SIR.
8. Install the door latch assembly. Refer to Latch Replacement - Front Door.
9. Install the window regulator. Refer to Window Regulator Replacement Front Door
10. Install the weatherstrip run channel. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement Front.
11. Install the window. Refer to Window Replacement Front Door.
12. Install the ORVM. Refer to Mirror Replacement.
13. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement 二 Front Door.
14. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement - Front Door in Entertainment.
15. Install the inside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Inside.
16. Install the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Front Door Window Belt Outer.
17. Install the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
18. Install the accessory switch panel Refer to Switch Replacement Door Lock and Side Window ■ Driver.
19. Enable the SIR. Refer to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 2 and to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 6 in SIR.

### DOOR REPLACEMENT SIDE REAR
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_d9207fae` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Removal Procedure

Remove the power window switch. Refer to Power Window Switch Replacement Rear Door (Chevrolet).
2. Remove the trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
3. Remove the inner window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear Door Window Belt Inner.
4. Remove the outer window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear Door Window Belt Outer.
5. Remove the upper sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear Door Upper.
6. Remove the inside release handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement Inside.
7. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
8. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Rear Door in Entertainment.

9. Remove the window. Refer to Window Replacement Rear Door(TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) or Window Replacement- Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).

10. Remove the stationary window and run channel assembly. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement - Rear (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement - Rear (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).

11. Remove the window regulator Refer to Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer, Envoy,Bravada)or Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).

12. Remove the door latch assembly Refer to Latch Replacement - Rear Door.

13. Remove the outside door handle Refer to Door Handle Replacement Rear Outside (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) or Door Handle Replacement Rear Outside (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).

15. Remove the wire harness from the door. Refer to Wiring Harness Replacement - Rear Door.

![](images/20c6b2c0ded1d7078b83fbf3f4cd90065ac5525690a68b7b3ed1233319b8bd81.jpg)
Fig. 34: Identifying Door To Hinge Plate Bolts Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

16. Remove the bolts that retain the door to the hinge plates.
17. Remove the door from the vehicle with an assistant.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_f1cfb4d1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/7495bf64d0f6ff8172926745affe89ae44274cdf45e7b9fba312c441b0e6dfa3.jpg)
Fig. 35: Identifying Door To Hinge Plate Bolts Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the door to the vehicle with al assistant.

NOTE: Refer toFastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

2. Apply Loctite? Blue GM P/N 12345382, or Canadian P/N 10953489, or equivalent to the fastener prior to installation.
3. Install the bolts that retain the door to the hinge plates.

Install the wire harness to the door. Refer to Wiring Harness Replacement- Rear Door.
5. Install the check link. Refer to Door Check Link Replacement.
6. Install the outside door handle Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Rear Outside (TrailBlazer EXT,Envoy XL) or Door Handle Replacement ： Rear Outside (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).
7. Install the door latch assembly. Refer to Latch Replacement Rear Door.
8. Install the window regulator Refer to Window Regulator Replacement- Rear Door (TrailBlazer,Envoy,Bravada) or Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL)
9. Install the stationary window and run channel assembly. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement Rear(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement - Rear (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).
10. Install the window. Refer to Window Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) or Window Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).
11. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Rear Door in Entertainment.
12. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
13. Install the inside release handle.Refer to Door Handle Replacement Inside.
14. Install the upper sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear Door Upper.
15. Install the outer window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear DoorWindow Belt Outer.
16. Install the inner window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear' Door Window Belt Inner.
17. Install the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
18. Install the power window switch. Refer to Power Window Switch Replacement Rear Door (Chevrolet).

### DOOR CHECKLINK REPLACEMENT
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_0c588f2c` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

Removal Procedure

Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side FrontDoor or Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the front half of the water deflector in order to expose the door access hole.
3 Remove the bolt retaining the front lower window run channel Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel A Assembly Replacement Front.

![](images/29c5fde5bae88830c28b1016263f4ac4928ebfefe9fb04179f43855cbbdf771f.jpg)

### Fig. 36: Identifying Check Link To Pillar Bolts Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_901db4a7` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Remove the 2 bolts that retain the check link to the pillar.

![](images/744211659bf36c6ce03c791532d645a942759693c1bade508d6b9fcac51a36ff.jpg)
Fig. 37: Identifying Rubber Dust Boot Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

5. Remove the rubber dust boot from the check link in order to gain access to the door retaining nuts.

![](images/13c7ac68f4060580e07647a6147c6fe3073c1c6bb94c5f739da61d23b3835882.jpg)
Fig. 38: Locating Check Link Retaining Nuts Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

6. Remove the 2 check link retaining nuts from the door.
7. Remove the check link assembly through the door access hole.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_f17f20af` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/2bf75463a311218cf2fd14f534022b2db2a1a8a2a6e0d66008a92fb7be3b280b.jpg)
Fig. 39: Locating Check Link Retaining Nuts Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the check link assembly through the door access hole. Ensure that the position arrow iS pointing forward.

### NOTE: Refer toFastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_cff7ea9c` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

2. Install the 2 check link retaining nuts to the door.

Tighten: Tighten the nuts to 7 N.m (62 Ib in).

![](images/37498b0a3cd8f98bf4289166ae12ee2fb8f2daa82bb61017af49f3956e3ef216.jpg)
Fig. 40: Identifying Rubber Dust Boot Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Install the rubber dust boot to the check link.

![](images/03fb870bef61aa965eba4ff70377892dacfecce14747088aa8e49e58ac7b6e7b.jpg)

### Fig. 41: Identifying Check Link To Pillar Bolts Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_99730127` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Install the 2 bolts that retain the check link to the pillar.

Tighten: Tighten the bolts to 15 N.m (11 1b ft).

5. Install the front lower window run channel. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement Front.

6. Install and fully seat the water deflector.

7. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door Ol Trim Panel Replacement - Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_fbec6837` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Close the window to the full up position.
2. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

![](images/9b53dedbad3889e99dcdc32b69bb092eb6cb7a9cdbc902f4861559a77c452a33.jpg)

### Fig. 42: Removing/Installing Door Handle Front Outside Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_fc208fe5` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Remove the rear half of the water deflector from the door.
4. Release the upper lock rod retainer (1) from the rod.
5. Remove the lock rod from the handle assembly.
6. Release the lock cylinder rod retainer (2) from the lock rod, if equipped.

7. Remove the lock cylinder rod from the handle assembly, if equipped.

8. Remove the 2 door handle retaining nuts.

9. Remove the handle assembly from the door.

10. Remove the lock cylinder from the handle, if equipped. Refer to Lock Cylinder Replacement Door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_34885c9b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the lock cylinder to the handle, if equipped. Refer to Lock Cylinder Replacement Door.

![](images/6766049571d9c09e6a012fc14829dd5099b5d78c2d06ad1c1da1148794f454e6.jpg)
Fig. 43: Removing/Installing Door Handle Front Outside Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Install the upper lock rod to the handle assembly.
4. Install the lock rod retainer (1).
5. Install the lock cylinder rod to the handle assembly, if equipped.
6. Install the cylinder lock rod retainer (2）, if equipped.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_48275ef9` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

7. Install the 2 door handle retaining nuts.

Tighten: Tighten the nuts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

8. Attach the rear half of the water deflector to the door.
9. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

)OOR HANDLE REPLACEMENT- REAR OUTSIDE (TRAILBLAZER EXT, ENVOY XL)

### Fig. 44: Removing/Installing Door Handle- Rear Outside (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy ofGENERA
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_b445e2bb` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
3. Release the clip (1) retaining the lock rod (2) to the outside door handle.
4. Remove the lock rod (2) from the outside door handle.
5. Remove the nuts retaining the outside door handle to the door.
6. Remove the outside door handle from the door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_14af35d6` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/1577f2846e94e671bb3d0c4109cc49828fca7c83d564f630ff367c470a47a7bf.jpg)

Fig. 45: Removing/Installing Door Handle - Rear Outside(TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL)
Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

1. Install the outside door handle to the door.
2. Install the nuts retaining the outside door handle to the door.

Tighten: Tighten the nuts to 10 N.m (89 lb in).

3. Install the lock rod (2) to the outside door handle.
4. Install the clip (l) retaining the lock rod (2) to the outside door handle.
5. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.

![](images/c566e033984797a375edc2308e4493e580640e36995245ff48116a52032f529e.jpg)

6. Install the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

)OOR HANDLE REPLACEMENT REAR OUTSIDE (TRAILBLAZER, ENVOY, BRAVADA)

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ed6673ed` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
3. Release the clip that retains the lock rod to the handle.
4. Remove the lock rod from the handle
5. Remove the nut that retains the handle and bracket to the door.
6. Remove the bracket from the handle.

![](images/883ec5de46d72193e174dc80d3f41bd8264ec8edd549fafc9d0e22bbb6596b42.jpg)
Fig. 46: Removing/Installing Door Handle Rear Outside (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

![](images/51a2abed7652f63eb9c74b5c1992a231246ad0b265b6132d8f357f42421758f7.jpg)

7. Remove the handle from the door.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_52046534` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Install the nut that retains the handle to the bracket.

Tighten: Tighten the nut to 10 N.m (88 lb in).

![](images/1ee1238e372e39813fd7cfb32c097b14f7bb37e9a260fda3235e32bae1e629c1.jpg)

4. Install the lock rod to the handle.
5. Install the clip that retains the lock rod to the handle.
6. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement- Rear Door.
7. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6cf0278e` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door or Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

![](images/101149402169643a463a6d2fb631f772c4d00599297e4d9b77502af254e7cf07.jpg)
Fig. 48: Removing/Installing Inside Door Handle Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Remove the handle retaining bolt.
3. Release the handle locator tab from the locator cutout, by sliding the handle forward.
4. Release the rod retainer locks.
5. Remove the rods from the handle.

![](images/b21fc7ed715897d91b26590e0e505f252ee0976b11446eeb1372c30e7aa63318.jpg)

6. Remove the handle from the door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_a3a854ae` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/d18db8a466e384cd9dcbae0e53e78054c687813c3810f71789dafc5f12f15c7e.jpg)
Fig. 49: Removing/Installing Inside Door Handle Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Position the handle to the door.
2. Install the rods to the handle.
3 Install the rod retainer locks.
4. Insert the handle locator tab into the locator cutout and move the handle rearward,
indexing the fastener hole to the door.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_42eb388c` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5. Install the handle retaining bolt.

Tighten: Tighten bolt to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

![](images/a7a2fa476f0c2f334c8172c516de7956d213a9e608562841d4020bbed4abb846.jpg)

6. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door or Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

LOCK ACTUATOR REPLACEMENT. FRONT DOOR

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6fda9ac1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
3. Remove the door latch assembly Refer to Latch Replacement Front Door.

![](images/d2d1bfc596f0070a4dda906e33b3e14a318ad79e16824127116122fb68828883.jpg)
Fig. 50: View Of Lock Actuator To Latch Assembly Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

4. Remove the 2 screws that retain the actuator (1)to the lock.

![](images/9a7f3a48730329a898b6e55bcd29d72afc7f4b790ece836772c0b479f11c0c17.jpg)

### Fig. 51: View Of Lock Actuator Rubber Bumper In Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_c72983ec` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5. Simultaneously lift up on and rotate the actuator away from the latch in order to disengage the rubber bumper (1) from the latch.

6. Remove the actuator from the latch.

### Fig. 52: View Of Lock Actuator Rubber Bumper In Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_0932ccb0` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the rubber bumper(1） to the latch.
2. Rotate the actuator toward the latch in order to fully engage the rubber bumper into the latch .

### IMPORTANT: Ensure that the first tooth of the gear on the actuator and the first tooth of the gear o
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_f4a8496c` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

properly.

![](images/67f50d051cdbdba3c494dcf14378de0f6df9e67c976d53ea8686264e7e58edc2.jpg)
Fig. 53: Aligning Actuator & Latch Gear Teeth Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Install the actuator to the top of the latch.

![](images/35e5f67b242db195e31b26c87f24cd92618d659864e1774a5c3bd3cb2ee17c43.jpg)
Fig. 54: View Of Lock Actuator To Latch Assembly Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

4. Install the 2 screws that retain the actuator (1) to the lock.

Tighten: Tighten the screws to 0.75 N.m (6 1b in).

![](images/e878affe2672d4c87621090a187f80cab440ec199ddc059cf4bd331b45e2a4c3.jpg)

5. Install the lock to the door. Refer to Latch Replacement Front Door.
6. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
7. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_f0cdefb5` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
3. Remove the door latch assembly. Refer to Latch Replacement - Rear Door.

![](images/68e47d8cbc67bbd8679729c49fc8aca1ea4fce35aabd8a94342053b23b75031f.jpg)
Fig. 55: View Of Lock Actuator To Latch Assembly Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

4. Remove the 2 screws that retain the actuator (1)to the lock.

![](images/c7ec9113bfb742cf35e547b851d4b3b525e24d9be8242259eac0e3ec114664e5.jpg)

### Fig. 56: View Of Lock Actuator Rubber Bumper In Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_c72983ec` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5. Simultaneously lift up on and rotate the actuator away from the latch in order to disengage the rubber bumper (1) from the latch.

6. Remove the actuator from the latch.

### Fig. 57: View Of Lock Actuator Rubber Bumper In Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_0932ccb0` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the rubber bumper(1） to the latch.
2. Rotate the actuator toward the latch in order to fully engage the rubber bumper into the latch .

### IMPORTANT: Ensure that the first tooth of the gear on the actuator and the first tooth of the gear o
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6c0f8e38` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

properly.

![](images/b9630884406c6d843a2b68974f2f8d4983481472393fcc4438c98e58832da35e.jpg)
Fig. 58: Aligning Actuator & Latch Gear Teeth Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Install the actuator to the top of the latch.

![](images/f2e8eaa49d08b742bdc020ad5b7b2e3f5992c6e0f908676821226e4b4ac3b768.jpg)
Fig. 59: View Of Lock Actuator To Latch Assembly Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

4. Install the 2 screws that retain the actuator (1) to the lock.

Tighten: Tighten the screws to 0.75 N.m (6 1b in).

![](images/2250e0cf2bc37de82dc362ba37f448fdaf9014eb187d7cbb8720dafc9f709d25.jpg)

5. Install the lock to the door. Refer to Latch Replacement Rear Door.
6. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
7. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_985733b1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
2. Remove the water deflector from the door. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
3. Remove the outside handle from the door. Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Front Outside.

![](images/8bd3df3138406c9ebd595a52ce2d5c049180abbff459f288e7f159e826801471.jpg)

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_c7a9d60b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/323d30718d17113a8b7137a23fd8214a9754bb80b79ce2ef733a44061fcdc4d2.jpg)
Fig. 61: Installing Lock Cylinder To Door Handle Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the lock cylinder to the door handle.
2. Install the outside handle to the door. Refer to Door Handle Replacement - Front Outside.
3. Install the water deflector to the door. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Fror Door.

4. Install the door trim panel to the door. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_e226d87b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Close the window to the full up position.
2. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
3. Remove the rear half of the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.

![](images/eea9a322eb509f1269ca2edd0dddccebb620cdc303862b1e83c1caae1a232973.jpg)

### Fig. 62: Disconnecting Lock Rod From Outside Door Handle Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_beed5e01` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Disconnect the lock rod retaining clip from the outside door handle.
5. Remove the lock rod from the outside door handle
6. Disconnect the lock cylinder rod from the cylinder, if equipped.
7. Remove the inside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement Inside.
8. Remove the 3 screws that retain the latch to the door.
9. Position the latch in order to disconnect the electrical connectors asneeded.
10. Remove the latch from the door.

![](images/10442ad1bba400c893b13110f0763cc0edd3a698e936c7363f90d0be59921528.jpg)
Fig. 63: Locating Latch To Door Screws Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

![](images/9e579f4ae39a7cce0cb57a0eecdc2f8dbffdaadc1912a56a6de0aec17fa7f2a1.jpg)
Fig. 64: Noting Position Of Inside Handle Lock Rods Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

11. Noting the position of the inside handle lock rods,remove the inside handle lock rods from the latch.
12. Noting the position of the outside handle lock rods, remove the outside handle lock rods from the latch.
13. Remove the actuator from latch. Refer to Lock Actuator Replacement Front Door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_945da023` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the actuator to the latch. Refer to Lock Actuator Replacement Front Door.

![](images/210dbfca5124e22c7f5ed58fa2795d02dd6cb8251b64b9d5ccd2b7a57094bcfd.jpg)
Fig. 65: Noting Position Of Inside Handle Lock Rods Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Install the outside handle lock rods to the latch as previously noted.
3. Install the inside handle lock rods to the latch as previously noted.
4. Position the latch in order to connect the electrical connectors asneeded.
5. Install the latch to the door.

![](images/6b850e38918c8e00efa2a081ef40967c4c69385524002700002ec5957403ec16.jpg)
Fig. 66: Locating Latch To Door Screws Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6b9b46b1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Install the 3 screws that retain the latch to the door.

Tighten: Tighten the screws to 10 N.m (22 1b in).

7. Install the inside door handle. Refer to Door Handle Replacement Inside.

![](images/caf789c267d959f4b23c57cae2d4ceb03e8e35169b11bce721159e0bf7166588.jpg)

### Fig. 67: Connecting Lock Rod To Outside Door Handle Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_eddcbe1b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8. Connect the lock cylinder rod to the cylinder, if equipped.
9. Install the lock rod to the outside door handle.
10. Connect the lock rod retaining clip to the outside door handle.
11. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
12. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_bd236acc` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Close the window to the full up position.
2. Remove the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
3. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement 1 Rear Door.

![](images/c18f7a720a448d838695a2ae30c428cc2970374728421028b4eea3d3466aceb3.jpg)

### Fig. 68: Disconnecting Inside Door Handle Lock Rods From Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_9dc1abe9` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Remove the inside door handle lock rods from the latch.

![](images/d85f65c5d5d2a49c90089691ca1fe1aa011ed4bb05e55cff5cf28e9519037553.jpg)
Fig. 69: Identifying Lock Rod Connection Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

5. Disconnect the outside handle lock rod from the outside handle.

![](images/9c9784db7da92eb60eb8825a93a39e44b654bb0248abb1ec1afc62953781d76f.jpg)
Fig. 70: Removing Latch From Rear Door Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

6. Remove the 3 fasteners that retain the latch to the door.

7. Disconnect the electrical connectors from the latch.

8. Remove the latch from the door.

9. Remove the actuator from the latch. Refer to Lock Actuator Replacement Rear Door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ff95b81d` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the actuator to the latch Refer to Lock Actuator Replacement Rear Door.

![](images/e72372b36e68e2e73cdc6b7173e884ce068f52637aefc96cdf888ecdc60d6a08.jpg)
Fig. 71: Installing Latch To Rear Door Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Position the latch to the door.
3. Connect the electrical connectors to the latch.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_24d2a02e` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Install the 3 fasteners that retain the latch to the door.

Tighten: Tighten the fasteners to 10.0 N.m (22 lb in).

![](images/3f5e595f400852a97c51513b7ce5a30b547998c8a10f98e9f236b2f3e9522e98.jpg)
Fig. 72: Identifying Lock Rod Connection Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

5. Install the outside handle lock rod to the outside handle

![](images/0e0574cd14d8974c9e86fd7c0a502b06ee6f366b58782fe932987acd5f5d08e3.jpg)

### Fig. 73: Connecting Inside Door Handle Lock Rods To Latch Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_a1ddfc2b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Install the inside door handle lock rod to the latch.
7. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
8. Install the door trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_e652e786` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/4857d64c1896ebafe4999e04a3fbb652e144dfbc8aac1f5441849250b2e90eeb.jpg)
Fig. 74: Removing Striker & Spacer From Door Frame Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Open the door.
2. Mark the position of the striker on the door frame.
3. Remove the 2 bolts that retain the striker to the door frame.
4. Remove the strik er and spacer from the door frame.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_4464b039` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/7a7e36b3e17377ab1360c9110461b5a8e5ed035a5b1f870fab466ec97a086d1f.jpg)
Fig. 75: Installing Striker & Spacer To Door Frame Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the striker and spacer to the door frame.

IMPORTANT: Do not tighten the bolts at this time.

2. Install the bolts that retain the striker to the door frame.
3. Position the striker and spacer to the door frame as previously marked.

### Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_c437a74e` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Fully tighten the bolts that retain the striker to the door frame.

Tighten: Tighten the bolts to 25 N.m (18 1b ft).

5. Close the door.

LOCK STRIKER ADJUSTMENT -DOOR

### Adjustment Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_2e685c99` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Open the door.

![](images/3e4bbd515f8cb09cace705de6c5a3c29148f7031e4101f5bcd486c2b29391623.jpg)
Fig. 76: Removing Striker & Spacer From Door Frame Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Remove the lock striker from the door opening.
3. Close the door with the lock striker removed.
4. Verify proper door fit to the door opening and weatherstrips. Adjust the door frame as
necessary.
5. Install the lock striker to the door opening.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_df59dd7c` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/6fefb48b2e3a61d5f321ac756ebdc4490a8323d6dd782ab07ed0cee3416f341c.jpg)

6. Install the lock striker screws retaining the lock striker to the door opening.

Tighten: Tighten the lock striker screws only until the screw heads are flush to the lock striker plate, but still allowing the striker to move.

![](images/b0b1fe453c64207037f8a5568b379efa77a266e4ccedfe1d9816ce4683fbbeaa.jpg)

### Fig. 77: Loosening Lock Striker Backing Plate From Body Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_170a805f` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

7. Using a large flat-bladed tool, rock the striker up and down to loosen the lock striker backing plate from the body.

### door.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_4e2eea7a` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8. Close the door while holding the outside handle in the open position to align the lock striker. If necessary, adjust the lock striker position to allow the door to close with minimum effort.

9. Open the door.

10. Finish tightening the lock striker screws.

Tighten: Tighten the lock striker screws to 25 N.m (18 1b ft).

11. Close the door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_a2321509` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:electrical | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Disable the SIR. Refer to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 2 and to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 6 in SIR.
2. Remove the side front door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
3. Remove the front door speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
4. Remove the front water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
5. Disconnect the regulator motor electrical connector.
6. Disconnect the door latch assembly electrical connector.
7. Disconnect the electrical connector from the SIR side impact sensor.
8. Disconnect the body harness inline electrical connector by releasing the connector retaining tab located on the body side of the connector.
9. Remove the front door wiring harness electrical connector from the mounting bracket by releasing the mounting bracket retaining tab located on the hinge side of the connector.

![](images/d14b269a6a39a0a8248b2fa17fb56d758a7eb8bd455f4e0ab2dabb3976930520.jpg)
Fig. 78: Locating Body Harness Inline Electrical Connector Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

10. Remove the rubber grommet from the door access opening.

![](images/28c009289f5ebfa9a432d1cfa98991a0a2c3e77444a6fd3d20573b7d2fc6855c.jpg)
Fig. 79: Identifying Front Door Wiring Harness Routing Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

11. Note the routing of the front door wiring harness and release the wire harness attachments from the door.
12. Pass the front door wiring harness electrical connector through the access opening and remove the front door wiring harness from the door.

### Installation Proccdure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_99e83a20` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:electrical | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/67ef05e0a74528944776b201333af6c827366e7c1e86fbd44e59e724e2bfd9e6.jpg)
Fig. 80: Identifying Front Door Wiring Harness Routing Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Pass the front door wiring harness electrical connector through the access opening and position the front door wiring harness to the door as previously noted. 2. Ensure that the front door wiring harness attachments are fully seated.

![](images/7f574ee457d999385bfe4cbaacf2d0b1541c6ee0a75ccc0f300fb67051a4a287.jpg)
Fig. 81: Locating Body Harness Inline Electrical Connector Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Attach the rubber grommet to the door access opening. Ensure that the grommet is fully seated.

4. Install the front door wiring harness electrical connector to the mounting bracket. Ensure that the mounting bracket retaining tab is fully seated.
5. Connect the body harness inline electrical connector to the door wire harness electrical connector. Ensure that the retaining tab is fully seated
6. Connect the electrical connector to the SIR side impact sensor.
7. Connect the door latch assembly electrical connector.
8. Connect the regulator motor electrical connector.
9. Install the front door water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
10. Install the front door speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in

Entertainment

11. Install the side front door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
12. Enable the SIR. Refer to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 2 and to SIR Disabling and Enabling Zone 6 in SIR.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_3d1f7227` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
3. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Rear Door in Entertainment.
4. Disconnect the regulator motor electrical connector.
5. Disconnect the door latch assembly electrical connector.
6. Remove the second pillar trim. Refer to Garnish Molding Replacement Center
Pillar in Interior Trim.

![](images/10e02dd97b3fa26b0c9e39636510ac5dfe65f0bff22c7ea28cb12487a10c0b21.jpg)

### Fig. 82: Accessing Door Wire Harness Connector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_1dad6901` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

7. From the inside of the door, release the rubber conduit retainer from the door by releasing the retaining tabs.
8. Using the pillar access hole, remove the door harness connector retainer from the second pillar by releasing the retaining tabs.
9. Disconnect the 6-way and the 8-way electrical connectors from the door wire harness

connector.

![](images/b5566207092adf447f96c81972a70ca7d6d9bb47d510bcc37899a97ef7ec37b9.jpg)

### Fig. 83: Identifying Rear Door Wiring Harness Routing Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_a71e19b3` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

10. Note the routing of the wire harness and release the wire harness attachments from the door.
11. Remove the wire harness from the door.

### Installation Proccdure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_26ffeaa5` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/0af48d6008aba746c522c9c11014fe7c05db2ac124911d38db00d76f7b704f79.jpg)
Fig. 84: Identifying Rear Door Wiring Harness Routing Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install and route the wiring harness to the door as previously noted.
2. Ensure that the wiring harness attachments are fully seated.

![](images/695cbd7f1bc2f63d442b9c073f4554799e7de7e930ff31285cb68ceaec228991.jpg)

### Fig. 85: Accessing Door Wire Harness Connector Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_ca27cc36` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

3. Index the rubber conduit retainer to the door.
4. Seat the rubber conduit retainer to the door.
5. Connect the 6-way electrical connector to the upper portion of the door harness
connector retainer.
6. Connect the 8-way electrical connector to the lower portion of the door harness

connector retainer.

7 Align the door harness connector retainer to the second pillar.
8. Seat the door harness connector retainer retaining tabs to the second pillar.
9. Install the second pillar trim panel Refer to Garnish Molding Replacement - Center
Pillar in Interior Trim.
10. Connect the door latch assembly electrical connector.
11. Connect the regulator motor electrical connector.
12. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Rear Door in Entertainment.
13. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
14. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Rear Door.

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_7341b2b8` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door.
2. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement- Front Door in Entertainment.
3. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
4. Loosen the regulator window carrier bolts.
5. Raisc and support thc window.

![](images/eb46d88af9f4b35f01693d0dbac10c57a9c4965cbcdaf592b907205ced3e593c.jpg)
Fig. 86: Replacing Window Regulator Front Door Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

6. Disconnect the electrical connector from the regulator motor.
7. Loosen the upper 2 bolts that retain the regulator assembly to the door.
8. Remove the remaining 3 bolts that retain the window regulator assembly and the motor to the door.
9. Lift the regulator assembly upward in order to release the upper 2 bolts.
10. Rotate the upper portion of the regulator assembly forward.
11. Beginning with the lower portion, remove the regulator assembly through the door opening.
12. Place the regulator assembly on a prepared surface.

13. Remove the upper 2 bolts from the regulator assembly.

### Fig. 87: Replacing Window Regulator Front Door Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_1fd03f99` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Partially install the upper 2 bolts to the regulator assembly.
2. Beginning with the upper portion, install the regulator assembly through the door
opening in a semi-horizontal position.
3. Position the regulator assembly fully forward into the door cavity.
4. Rotate the regulator assembly to a vertical position.
5. Lower and hang the regulator assembly onto the door sheet metal using the upper 2

bolts that were previously installed.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_654872b3` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Install the bolt that retains the motor to the door.

Tighten: Tighten bolts to 9 N.m (80 Ib in).

7. Install the remaining bolts that retain the regulator assembly to the door.

Tighten: Tighten bolts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

8. Connect the electrical connector to the regulator motor.
9. Remove the window support and lower the window onto the regulator window carrier.
10. Loosely tighten the regulator window carrier bolts.
11. Operate the window upward to the full up position, ensuring the window remains in the
run channels.

Tighten: Tighten the regulator window carrier bolts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

12. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
13. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
14. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

WINDOW REGULATOR REPLACEMENT- REAR DOOR(TRAILBLAZER, ENVOY, BRAVADA)

### Removal Procedu re
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_9bddb3f1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
3. Remove the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement - Rear Door in Entertainment.
4. Loosen the regulator window carrier bolts.
5. Raise and support the window.
6. Disconnect the electrical connector from the regulator motor.
7. Remove the 3 bolts that retain the window regulator assembly to the door.
8. Remove the window regulator assembly from the door.

![](images/a703eb24c7b7463bcb607e12bd0261d040a0ebac68680fdd5934b5d83620a957.jpg)
Fig. 88: Replacing Window Regulator Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

### Fig. 89: Replacing Window Regulator Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTOR
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_cae4ea74` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the window regulator assembly to the door.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

2. Install the 3 bolts that retain the window regulator assembly to the door.

Tighten: Tighten the bolts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

3. Connect the electrical connector to the regulator motor.
4. Remove the window support and lower the window onto the regulator window carrier.

5. Loosely tighten the regulator window carrier bolts.

6. Operate the window upward to the full up position, ensuring the window remains in the run channels.

Tighten: Tighten the regulator window carrier bolts to 10 N.m (88 lb in).

7. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Rear Door in Entertainment.
8. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
9. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

WINDOW REGULATOR REPLACEMENT REAR DOOR(TRAILBLAZER EXT, ENVOY XL)

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_66840089` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Lower the window to the midway point (1), if possible

![](images/7564120e084c45b39e2046f93ad2b28f4eb2bc8b8a83f57cb87f46e7499c9953.jpg)
Fig. 90: Replacing Window Regulator Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

2. Remove the rear door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

3. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.

4. Loosen the regulator glass carrier bolts (2).

5. Slide the window to the full up position and tape it to the window frame.

6. Disconnect the electrical connector from the window regulator motor.

![](images/f451d323fe3d1987450d7a7f548ee4bb98bfdca2d3f986bfd97ae60efe1df37a.jpg)
Fig. 91: Locating Window Regulator Bolts Rear Door(TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

7. Disconnect the retainer (4) securing the regulator cable to the door.
8. Remove the 2 lower regulator retaining bolts (2,3).
9. Loosen the top regulator retaining bolt (1).

### IMPORTANT: If the regulator assembly will not operate, removing the top regulator retaining bolt (1)
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_2544c370` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

10. Remove the window regulator, lifting up and out from the keyhole slot (5) on the rear door inner panel and through the access hole.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_3a3bd0ca` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/21bbfd0763697faab48ffd4aeb4796dc7521973ade322ea95fbb9a4a40b87ea0.jpg)
Fig. 92: Locating Window Regulator Bolts Rear Door(TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the regulator to the door through the access hole.
2. Install the top regulator retaining bolt (1) through the keyhole slot (5) in the rear door inner panel.
3. Install the 2 lower regulator retaining bolts (2, 3).

### Tighten:
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_90a57fbc` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Tighten the lower regulator retaining bolt (3) to 10 N.m (89 lb in).
2. Tighten the upper regulator retaining bolt (1) to 10 N.m (89 Ib in).
3. Tighten the middle regulator retaining bolt (2) to 10 N.m (89 lb in).
5. Connect the regulator cable retainer (4) on the door.
6. Connect the electrical connector to the rear window regulator motor.

![](images/3da6515e31d9f14f68a51fc5ba40f5b411cc3b76188a1ae7c8461060113e5c88.jpg)

Fig. 93: Replacing Window Regulator - Rear Door(TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

7. Remove the tape and position the rear window to the regulator glass carrier plate.

![](images/68a902cb40174660acdda959cdec0088cbca6953e086327f0fce7f8066fb2787.jpg)

8. Loosely tighten the regulator glass carrier bolts (2).

9. Operate the window upward to the full up position ensuring the window remains in the run channels.

Tighten: Tighten the regulator glass carrier bolts (2) to 10 N.m (89 ft in).

10. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
11. Install the trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

WINDOW REGULATOR MOTOR REPLACEMENT- DOOR

Removal Procedure

![](images/4df9565ca4c43d342b16bed2a0b22bd1d5915de6a7c2d839d75c0cac86750293.jpg)

1. Remove the window regulator. Refer to Window Regulator Replacement Front Door, Window Regulator Replacement- Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).
2. Remove the bolts retaining the window regulator motor to the window regulator.
3. Remove the window regulator motor from the window regulator.

### Installation Proccdure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_022346c8` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:general | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/5132a87840daa1eb96f4aa42db6125d0faf77ad5a0679846ba4a6477a667727d.jpg)
Fig. 95: Locating Window Regulator Motor Bolts Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Install the window regulator motor to the window regulator.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and

### Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_e4e3fe22` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

2. Install the bolts retaining the window regulator motor to the window regulator

Tighten: Tighten the window regulator motor retaining bolts to 5 N.m (9 lb in)

3. Install the window regulator. Refer to Window Regulator Replacement - Front Door, Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Regulator Replacement - Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT,Envoy XL).

WINDOW REPLACEMENT FRONT DOOR

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_a9135f8e` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.
2. Remove the door speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
3. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
4. Remove the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Front Do01 Window Belt Outer.

CAUTION: Disconnect the power window switch when working inside the driver's door. When operated, the Express Down Feature allows the door window to drop very quickly, without stopping, which could cause personal injury.

![](images/d1a222820bc11c45ec996966a7780b47ec5cd95fd3357c204d3523afaabc9931.jpg)

### Fig. 96: Removing Front Door Window Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_1779ac38` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5. Adjust the window to the remove/load position (2).
6. Mark the location of the window at the regulator clamps.
7. Loosen the 2 regulator clamp bolts.

CAUTION: When working with any type of glass or sheet metal with exposed or rough edges, wearapproved safety glasses and gloves in order to reduce the chance of personal injury.

8. In order to remove the window out of the frame, carefully grasp and tilt the window as

![](images/74c4669feb8a2eed900d77f7536a1e9c1f2378fe765318a0060418984b5e419b.jpg)

you guide the window up and forward. 9. Remove the window from the door.

### Fig. 97: Installing Front Door Window Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_32c6b624` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the window to the door with the regulator adjusted to the remove/load position (2).

2. Tilt the window forward and carefully guide the window into the rear run channel.

3. Position the window to an upright position. Ensure that the window 1S seated into the run channels.

4. Lower and position the window into the regulator clamps at the alignment marks previously made.
5. Loosely tighten the regulator clamp bolts.

IMPORTANT: Do not operate the regulator motor without supporting the window. Ensure that the window remains in the run channels when operating the regulator motor.

6. Carefully move the regulator upward for short slow intervals, while ensuring that the window remains in the run channels.

7. Position the window to a fully secure position (l) into the upper door weatherstrip.

NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.

8. Tighten the 2 regulator clamp nuts.

Tighten: Tighten the nuts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

9. Install the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Front Door Window Belt Outer.
10. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
11. Install the door speaker Refer to Speaker Replacement Front Door in Entertainment.
12. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door.

WINDOW REPLACEMENT REAR DOOR (TRAILBLAZER EXT, ENVOY XL)

Removal Procedure

![](images/c48b9dcf481f371c4efa4ef96f30745111e0c95d41abcb46efcbe754c3da8d2b.jpg)
Fig. 98: View Of Door With Trim Panel Removed Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. Remove the rear door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
3. Remove the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear Door Window Belt Outer.
4. Remove the window regulator. Refer to Window Regulator Replacement - Rear Door (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).
5. Carefully lower the window (3) down into the door.
6. Remove the window weatherstrip run channel assembly. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement- Rear (TrailBlazer, Envoy,

### Fig. 100: Installing Rear Door Window (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_96fc37b1` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the rear door window between the inner and the outer door panels.
2. Position the rear door window in the bottom of the door.

![](images/d2b1f365aba2728a8f9f23f81553be2c536151b8eef6255f3a3b14296d081374.jpg)
Fig. 101: View Of Door With Trim Panel Removed Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.

3. Install the window weatherstrip run channel assembly. Refer to Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement - Rear (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Replacement Rear (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).

4. Align the window (3) into the weatherstrip run channel (4) while raising the window into the full up position.

5. Tape the window to the window frame in the full up position.

6. Install the window regulator Refer to Window Regulator Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) or Window Regulator Replacement - Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL).

IMPORTANT: Do NOT allow the sealing lips on the outer belt sealing strip to roll over and point downwards. If this condition is present the window will not raise properly.

7. Install the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear Door Window Belt Outer. 8. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door. 9. Install the trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

WINDOW REPLACEMENT / REAR DOOR (TRAILBLAZER, ENVOY,BRAVADA)

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_1b73a47f` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door. 2. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door. 3. Remove the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear Door Window Belt Outer.

![](images/519c303d0cd082e85a82247ac8e808b35053d2d253c401d8e52ec28625d102c4.jpg)

### Fig. 102: Removing Rear Door Window (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_2021db79` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Adjust the window to the remove/load position (2).
5. Mark the location of the window at the regulator clamps.
6. Loosen the 2 regulator clamp bolts.

CAUTION: When working with any type of glass or sheet metal with exposed or rough edges, wearapproved safety glasses and gloves in order to reduce the chance of personal injury.

7. In order to remove the window out of the frame, carefully grasp and tilt the window as you guide the window up and forward.

8. Remove the window from the door.

### Installation Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_6fab4470` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

![](images/bd4f431896b6290170bc9938e4b01573aaec3df84decfe42c01cc4df3ffccb56.jpg)
Fig. 103: Installing Rear Door Window (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.

1. With the regulator in the remove/load position (2), install the window to the door.
2. Tilt the window forward and carefully guide the window into the rear run channel.
3. Position the window to an upright position. Ensure that the window is seated into the run channels.
4. Lower and position the window into the regulator clamps at the alignment marks previously made.
5. Loosely tighten the regulator clamp bolts.

### IMPORTANT: Do not operate the regulator motor without supporting the window. Ensure that the window 
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_61954dfa` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Carefully move the regulator upward for short slow intervals, while ensuring that the window remains in the run channels. 7. Position the window to a fully secure position (1) into the upper door weatherstrip.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_e41f5a58` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8. Tighten the 2 regulator clamp nuts.

Tighten: Tighten the nuts to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

9. Lower the window to the full down position.
10. Install the outer belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Front Door
Window Belt Outer.
11. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Front Door.
12. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Front Door.

WINDOW WEATHERSTRIP RUN CHANNELASSEMBLY REPLACEMENT FRONT

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_7a5452ef` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement - Side Front Door.
2. Remove the speaker Refer to Speaker Replacement 1 Front Door in Entertainment.
3. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
4. Remove the window. Refer to Window Replacement - Front Door.
5. Remove the outside rearview mirror. Refer to Mirror Replacement.

![](images/def5a94f803806aaff781ae7e9800d43906f91ab4001f56f055e20ff47a162e1.jpg)

### Fig. 104: Removing Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Front Courtesy ofGENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_9c7685c3` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Remove the 2 bolts that retain the weatherstrip/run channel to the door.
7. Pull the weatherstrip/run channel away from the window frame opening.
8. Carefully twist and pull up on the weatherstrip/run channel, in order remove the
weatherstrip/run channel froim the top of the door frame.
9. Remove the weatherstrip/run channel from the door.

### Fig. 105: Installing Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Front Courtesy of GENERAL MOTORS CORP.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_fb7ff742` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the weatherstrip/run channel to the door.
2. Position the tabs on the weatherstrip/run channel into the slots in the door.
3. Press the weatherstrip/run channel into the window frame opening.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_3d05bbdd` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

4. Install the 2 retaining bolts.

5. Install the outside rearview mirror. Refer to Mirror Replacement.
6. Install the window. Refer to Window Replacement - Front Door.
7. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Front Door.
8. Install the speaker. Refer to Speaker Replacement - Front Door in Entertainment.
9. Install the door trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement □ Side Front Door.

WINDOW WEATHERSTRIP RUN CHANNELASSEMBLY REPLACEMENT-REAR(TRAILBLAZER, ENVOY,BRAVADA)

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_798bb9ab` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Remove the trim panel Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.
2. Remove the inner window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip ReplacementRear Door Window Belt Inner.
3. Remove the outer window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement. RearDoorWindow Belt Outer.
4. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.
5. Remove the window. Refer to Window Replacement - Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT. Envoy XL) or Window Replacement - Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).

![](images/d4fa5a44b413eb2c19a374c865284dfe8782516614e95fd82ea4ee4fb483e3d6.jpg)

### Fig. 106: Removing Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly - Rear (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Cou
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_14371f36` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

6. Remove the bolt that retains the weatherstrip run channel to the door.
7. Pull the weatherstrip/run channel from the window frame opening.
8. carefully release the double sided tape ataching the molding to the outside of the door.
9. Carefully twist the weatherstrip run channel and pull up at the top of the door frame.

### Fig. 107: Installing Window Weatherstrip Run Channel Assembly Rear (TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada) Cou
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_770dbc5e` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

1. Install the weatherstrip/run channel to the door.
2. Press the weatherstrip/run channel into the window frame opening.
3. Position the tabs on the weatherstrip/run channel into the slots on the door.
4. Attach the molding to the outside of the door with double sided tape.

### NOTE: Refer to Fastener Notice in Cautions and Notices.
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_d696404a` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

5. Install the weatherstrip/run channel retaining bolt.

Tighten: Tighten the bolt to 10 N.m (88 1b in).

6. Install the window. Refer to Window Replacement Rear Door (TrailBlazer EXT, Envoy XL) or Window Replacement - Rear Door(TrailBlazer, Envoy, Bravada).

7. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement- Rear Door.

8. Install the outer window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear DoorWindow Belt Outer.
9. Install the inner window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear DoorWindow Belt Inner.
10. Install the trim panel. Refer to Trim Panel Replacement Side Rear Door.

WINDOW WEATHERSTRIP RUN CHANNELASSEMBLY REPLACEMENT-REAR(TRAILBLAZER EXT, ENVOY XL)

### Removal Procedure
`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_70d46e8b` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

2. Remove the inner window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement Rear Door Window Belt Inner.
3. Remove the outer window belt sealing strip. Refer to Sealing Strip Replacement - Rear Door Window Belt Outer.
4. Remove the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement Rear Door.

![](images/9c5a81439b44a2e387fcb3833d43a92c9765ad0fb71ac08978003650b75c154e.jpg)

`chevrolet_trailblazer_en_68731f46` | trailblazer | pdf_manual | tier:4 | lang:en | layer:body | p:1 | doc_type:service_manual

8. Install the water deflector. Refer to Water Deflector Replacement - Rear Door.
